# M7c netgraph probe, part 2 (SR-02 anti-spoofing, ADR 0010 amendment y), hearst, 2026-11-21.
# Probe only: no Skep code change. Proxmox snapshot p7-plus-b61e578 (Andy).
# Andy's decisions for this run: cleanup option (a) (netgraph, ng_socket, ng_ether stay
# loaded until Andy reboots hearst; ng_bpf unloaded; every node and hook removed); the jail
# effect of ng_socket accepted and measured; one bare tap; vlanfilter on bridge1 only.
# hearst: 15.1-RELEASE-p7, Skep b61e578 (unchanged). Test jails m7c-attacker
# (d9f783dc..., 192.0.2.10/24, MAC 02:5e:00:0a:00:10, host end epair0a) and m7c-victim
# (0476baf3..., 192.0.2.20/24, MAC 02:5e:00:0a:00:20, host end epair1a), static NICs on
# bridge1 (no uplink), made and deleted through the API as s7oper (--cacert +
# --pinnedpubkey, never -k; password from the read loader, token never printed).
# Forged MAC 02:5e:00:00:00:01; forged sources 192.0.2.50 and 198.51.100.10.
# Senders: rawping (d0ef1720...) and rawl2 (60e81c76..., with -vlan) in the attacker jail
# only; bpf unhidden in the attacker jail's own /dev only (as in part 1, models a DHCP
# jail). The victim jail kept devfs ruleset 5 (no bpf) and was used for test 6 (netgraph
# from a static jail), with the template's own /usr/sbin/ngctl and nghook.
# Filter: tools/ngbpfgen output (ngctl -f), layout <if>:lower -> [jail] ng_bpf skf_<if>
# [bridge] -> <if>:upper. Counters: ngctl msg skf_<if>: getstats "jail" / "bridge".
# Evidence: tcpdump -n -e on epair0a (sent; bpf runs before ng_ether), bridge1 (bridge
# input: a frame dropped by ng_bpf never gets there) and epair1a (delivered to the
# victim). tcpdump timestamps are hearst local time (UTC-5); ==== markers are UTC.
# Script mistakes kept in the record: in 12 (first static run) the counter lines are
# missing (getstats needs the hook name quoted, "jail"; fixed for 14 onwards and the
# static set was repeated with counters as 17); getprogram in 14's show() printed
# nothing for the same reason (16/15 show it). V4 (VID 0) is not supported by rawl2
# (exit 1, nothing sent) and was dropped from the set.
# Full logs: ~/skep-test-logs/20261121T214311Z-hearst-m7c-ng-*.log

######## harness file: ng-tests.sh
#!/bin/sh
# M7c netgraph probe test harness (runs on hearst as aba, uses sudo).
# Usage: sh ng-tests.sh LABEL [test ...]   (default: the A-F set)
# Evidence per test: tcpdump -n -e on epair0a (attacker host end: what the
# attacker sent; bpf runs before ng_ether), bridge1 (bpf on the bridge's
# input: with ng_bpf on epair0a a dropped frame never reaches BRIDGE_INPUT,
# so this capture is meaningful here, unlike part 1) and epair1a (victim
# host end: what the bridge delivered). Then the victim's ARP table and the
# ng_bpf hook counters (before/after) when the node exists.
LABEL=$1; shift
JA=${JA:?}; JV=${JV:?}
AM=02:5e:00:0a:00:10; VM=02:5e:00:0a:00:20; FM=02:5e:00:00:00:01
NODE=${NODE:-skf_epair0a}
D=/tmp/m7c-ng/cap; mkdir -p $D

cap_start() {
	for i in epair0a bridge1 epair1a; do
		sudo tcpdump -l -n -e -tttt -i $i 'not stp' > $D/$1-$i.txt 2>/dev/null &
	done
	sleep 1.5
}
cap_stop() {
	sleep 2
	sudo pkill -f "tcpdump -l -n -e -tttt -i" ; sleep 0.5
	for i in epair0a bridge1 epair1a; do
		n=$(grep -c '^20' $D/$1-$i.txt)
		echo "  -- $i: $n frame(s)"; grep '^20' $D/$1-$i.txt | sed 's/^/     /'
	done
}
ngstat() {
	if sudo ngctl info $NODE: >/dev/null 2>&1; then
		for h in jail bridge; do
			sudo ngctl msg $NODE: getstats \"$h\" 2>&1 | sed -n 's/^Args:[[:space:]]*/  ng '"$1 $h"': /p'
		done
	else
		echo "  ng $1: no node $NODE"
	fi
}
clear_arp() {
	sudo jexec $JA arp -d -a >/dev/null 2>&1; sudo jexec $JV arp -d -a >/dev/null 2>&1
}
varp() { echo "  victim arp:"; sudo jexec $JV arp -an | sed 's/^/     /'; }
run() { echo "  \$ $*"; "$@" > $D/out 2>&1; rc=$?; sed 's/^/     /' $D/out; echo "     (exit $rc)"; }

t_D1() { clear_arp; cap_start D1; run sudo jexec $JA ping -c 2 -t 3 192.0.2.20; cap_stop D1; varp; }
t_D2() { clear_arp; cap_start D2; run sudo jexec $JV ping -c 2 -t 3 192.0.2.10; cap_stop D2; varp; }
t_A1() { cap_start A1; run sudo jexec $JA /root/rawl2 -if skep0 -mode icmp -eth-src $FM -eth-dst $VM -src 192.0.2.10 -dst 192.0.2.20 -count 2; cap_stop A1; }
t_A2() { clear_arp; cap_start A2
	run sudo jexec $JA ifconfig skep0 ether $FM
	run sudo jexec $JA ping -c 2 -t 3 192.0.2.20
	run sudo jexec $JA ifconfig skep0 ether $AM
	cap_stop A2; varp; sudo jexec $JA ifconfig skep0 | grep -w ether | sed 's/^/  attacker now:/'; clear_arp; }
t_B1() { clear_arp; cap_start B1; run sudo jexec $JA /root/rawping -src 192.0.2.50 -dst 192.0.2.20 -count 2; cap_stop B1; }
t_B2() { cap_start B2; run sudo jexec $JA /root/rawping -src 198.51.100.10 -dst 192.0.2.20 -count 2; cap_stop B2; }
t_C1() { clear_arp; cap_start C1; run sudo jexec $JA /root/rawl2 -if skep0 -mode arp -op reply -eth-src $AM -eth-dst $VM -sha $AM -spa 192.0.2.50 -tha $VM -tpa 192.0.2.20 -count 2; cap_stop C1; varp; }
t_C2() { clear_arp; sudo jexec $JV ping -c 1 -t 2 192.0.2.10 >/dev/null 2>&1; echo "  victim arp before:"; sudo jexec $JV arp -an | sed 's/^/     /'
	cap_start C2; run sudo jexec $JA /root/rawl2 -if skep0 -mode arp -op reply -eth-src $AM -eth-dst $VM -sha $FM -spa 192.0.2.10 -tha $VM -tpa 192.0.2.20 -count 2; cap_stop C2; varp; clear_arp; }
t_C3() { clear_arp; cap_start C3
	run sudo jexec $JA ifconfig skep0 inet 192.0.2.50/32 alias
	run sudo jexec $JA ping -c 2 -t 3 -S 192.0.2.50 192.0.2.20
	run sudo jexec $JA ifconfig skep0 inet 192.0.2.50 -alias
	cap_stop C3; varp; sudo jexec $JA ifconfig skep0 | grep -w inet | sed 's/^/  attacker now:/'; clear_arp; }
t_C4() { clear_arp; cap_start C4; run sudo jexec $JA /root/rawl2 -if skep0 -mode arp -op request -eth-src $AM -sha $AM -spa 192.0.2.20 -tpa 192.0.2.20 -count 2; cap_stop C4; varp; }
t_C5() { clear_arp; cap_start C5
	run sudo jexec $JA arp -s 192.0.2.50 $FM pub
	run sudo jexec $JV ping -c 1 -t 2 192.0.2.50
	run sudo jexec $JA arp -d 192.0.2.50
	cap_stop C5; varp; clear_arp; }
t_E1() { cap_start E1; run sudo jexec $JA /root/rawl2 -if skep0 -mode dhcp -eth-src $AM -count 2; cap_stop E1; }
t_E2() { cap_start E2; run sudo jexec $JA /root/rawl2 -if skep0 -mode dhcp -eth-src $FM -count 2; cap_stop E2; }
t_E3() { clear_arp; cap_start E3; run sudo jexec $JA /root/rawl2 -if skep0 -mode arp -op reply -eth-src $AM -eth-dst $VM -sha $FM -spa 192.0.2.10 -tha $VM -tpa 192.0.2.20 -count 2; cap_stop E3; varp; clear_arp; }
t_F1() { cap_start F1; run sudo jexec $JA /root/rawl2 -if skep0 -mode ipv6 -eth-src $AM -count 2; cap_stop F1; }
# T5 from the M7b spoofing tests: an alias in the jail, then traffic from it.
t_T5() { clear_arp; cap_start T5
	run sudo jexec $JA ifconfig skep0 inet 192.0.2.60/24 alias
	run sudo jexec $JA ping -c 2 -t 3 -S 192.0.2.60 192.0.2.20
	run sudo jexec $JA ifconfig skep0 inet 192.0.2.60 -alias
	cap_stop T5; varp; clear_arp; }
# VLAN: V1 one tag, valid inner; V2 one tag, forged inner MAC; V3 two tags,
# valid inner; V4 priority tag (VID 0), valid inner.
t_V1() { cap_start V1; run sudo jexec $JA /root/rawl2 -if skep0 -mode icmp -vlan 5 -eth-src $AM -eth-dst $VM -src 192.0.2.10 -dst 192.0.2.20 -count 2; cap_stop V1; }
t_V2() { cap_start V2; run sudo jexec $JA /root/rawl2 -if skep0 -mode icmp -vlan 5 -eth-src $FM -eth-dst $VM -src 192.0.2.10 -dst 192.0.2.20 -count 2; cap_stop V2; }
t_V5() { cap_start V5; run sudo jexec $JA /root/rawl2 -if skep0 -mode icmp -vlan 1 -eth-src $AM -eth-dst $VM -src 192.0.2.10 -dst 192.0.2.20 -count 2; cap_stop V5; }
t_V3() { cap_start V3; run sudo jexec $JA /root/rawl2 -if skep0 -mode icmp -vlan 5,7 -eth-src $AM -eth-dst $VM -src 192.0.2.10 -dst 192.0.2.20 -count 2; cap_stop V3; }
t_V4() { cap_start V4; run sudo jexec $JA /root/rawl2 -if skep0 -mode icmp -vlan 0 -eth-src $AM -eth-dst $VM -src 192.0.2.10 -dst 192.0.2.20 -count 2; cap_stop V4; }

ALL="D1 D2 A1 A2 B1 B2 C1 C2 C3 C4 C5 E1 E2 F1 T5 V1 V2 V3"
[ $# -eq 0 ] && set -- $ALL
echo "######## tests [$LABEL] $(date -u +%FT%TZ): $*"
for t in "$@"; do
	echo "==== $LABEL $t $(date -u +%T)"
	ngstat before
	t_$t
	ngstat after
done
echo "######## end [$LABEL] $(date -u +%FT%TZ)"

######## harness file: d-depth.sh

st start

sudo ifconfig bridge1 inet 192.0.2.1/24 alias

{ set +x; } 2>/dev/null; st after-jail-to-host; set -x

{ set +x; } 2>/dev/null; st after-host-to-jail; set -x

{ set +x; } 2>/dev/null; st after-broadcast; set -x

sudo jexec $JA arp -an
sudo ifconfig bridge1 inet 192.0.2.1 -alias
ifconfig bridge1 | grep inet
{ set +x; } 2>/dev/null
echo "######## NG S3: cost: latency and TCP throughput attacker->victim, filter present, $(date -u +%FT%TZ)"
for i in 1 2 3; do
  sudo jexec $JV sh -c 'nc -l 192.0.2.20 5001 > /dev/null' &
  sleep 1
  sudo jexec $JA sh -c 'dd if=/dev/zero bs=64k count=16000 2>/tmp/dd.err | nc -N 192.0.2.20 5001; tail -1 /tmp/dd.err; rm /tmp/dd.err'
  wait
done
sudo jexec $JA ping -q -c 1000 -i 0.002 192.0.2.20 | tail -2
st after-cost

######## harness file: failopen.sh

T="env $E sh /tmp/m7c-ng/ng-tests.sh"
show() { echo "+ ngctl show epair0a:"; sudo ngctl show epair0a: | sed 's/^/    /'; if sudo ngctl info skf_epair0a: >/dev/null 2>&1; then echo "+ ngctl show skf_epair0a:"; sudo ngctl show skf_epair0a: | sed 's/^/    /'; for h in jail bridge; do echo "+ getprogram $h:"; sudo ngctl msg skf_epair0a: getprogram \"$h\" 2>&1 | sed -n 's/^Args:/   /p;s/^ngctl:/    ngctl:/p'; done; else echo "    (no node skf_epair0a)"; fi; }

######## harness file: health.sh
echo "######## NG H1: reinstall the static filter, then health-check primitives and timing, $(date -u +%FT%TZ)"
set -x

sudo ngctl msg epair0a: getenaddr

sudo ngctl msg epair0a: getautosrc

{ set +x; } 2>/dev/null

t0=$(date +%s.%N 2>/dev/null || date +%s)
/usr/bin/time -p sh -c 'i=0; while [ $i -lt 100 ]; do sudo ngctl show epair0a: >/dev/null; sudo ngctl msg skf_epair0a: getprogram "\"jail\"" >/dev/null; i=$((i+1)); done'
echo "timing: same 200 messages in one ngctl -f batch"
i=0; : > /tmp/m7c-ng/batch; while [ $i -lt 100 ]; do echo 'show epair0a:' >> /tmp/m7c-ng/batch; echo 'msg skf_epair0a: getprogram "jail"' >> /tmp/m7c-ng/batch; i=$((i+1)); done
/usr/bin/time -p sudo ngctl -f /tmp/m7c-ng/batch > /dev/null
rm /tmp/m7c-ng/batch

######## harness file: injail.sh
JA=skep-d9f783dc-1f9d-43ed-af99-f83f6ee79477; JV=skep-0476baf3-98b7-417e-9435-fdae0d9d41f3
RV=/skeptest/skep/jail/0476baf3-98b7-417e-9435-fdae0d9d41f3
D=/tmp/m7c-ng/cap
r() { echo "  [victim jail root]\$ $*"; sudo jexec $JV sh -c "$*" 2>&1 | sed 's/^/     /'; echo "     (exit $?)"; }
cap_start() { for i in epair1a bridge1 epair0a; do sudo tcpdump -l -n -e -tttt -i $i 'not stp' > $D/$1-$i.txt 2>/dev/null & done; sleep 1.5; }
cap_stop() { sleep 2; sudo pkill -f "tcpdump -l -n -e -tttt -i"; sleep 0.5
	for i in epair1a bridge1 epair0a; do n=$(grep -c '^20' $D/$1-$i.txt); echo "  -- $i: $n frame(s)"; grep '^20' $D/$1-$i.txt | sed 's/^/     /'; done; }
aarp() { echo "  attacker arp:"; sudo jexec $JA arp -an | sed 's/^/     /'; }
inject() { # label
	sudo jexec $JA arp -d -a >/dev/null 2>&1; sudo jexec $JA ping -c 1 -t 2 192.0.2.20 >/dev/null 2>&1; aarp
	cap_start $1
	r 'nghook skep0: lower < /root/inj-forged-mac-icmp.bin > /dev/null'
	r 'nghook skep0: lower < /root/inj-arp-forged-sha.bin > /dev/null'
	cap_stop $1; aarp; }
echo "######## NG J0: what the static victim jail's root can do with netgraph (no bpf, devfs ruleset 5), $(date -u +%FT%TZ)"
sudo install -m 0644 /tmp/m7c-ng/inj-forged-mac-icmp.bin /tmp/m7c-ng/inj-arp-forged-sha.bin $RV/root/
sudo jls -j $JV -n devfs_ruleset vnet allow.raw_sockets enforce_statfs securelevel 2>&1 | sed 's/^/  jail params: /'
r 'id; ls /dev | grep -c bpf; which ngctl nghook'
r 'ngctl list'
r 'ngctl types'
r 'ngctl show skep0:'
r 'ngctl msg skep0: getifname; ngctl msg skep0: getenaddr; ngctl msg skep0: getifindex'
echo "  -- reach the host-side nodes (by name and by host node ID):"
r 'ngctl show skf_epair0a:'
r 'ngctl show epair0a:'
r 'ngctl show epair1a:'
r 'ngctl show vtnet0:'
r 'ngctl show [5]:'
r 'ngctl show [1]:'
r 'ngctl msg skf_epair0a: setprogram { thisHook=\"jail\" ifMatch=\"bridge\" ifNotMatch=\"\" bpf_prog_len=1 bpf_prog=[ { code=6 jt=0 jf=0 k=4294967295 } ] }'
r 'ngctl shutdown skf_epair0a:'
echo "  -- create nodes of a type that is not loaded (needs kldload) and of loaded types:"
r 'ngctl mkpeer skep0: tee lower left'
r 'ngctl mkpeer skep0: ksocket lower inet/raw/icmp'
r 'ngctl mkpeer . bpf x jail; ngctl list; ngctl shutdown .:x 2>/dev/null; ngctl list | head -1'
r 'ngctl mkpeer . ether x lower'
echo "  host view while that ran:"; sudo ngctl list | sed 's/^/     /'
sudo ngctl show epair0a: | sed 's/^/     /'
echo "######## NG J1: inject forged frames through skep0:lower, NO filter on the victim's port epair1a, $(date -u +%FT%TZ)"
sudo ngctl show epair1a: | sed 's/^/  host: /'
inject J1
echo "######## NG J2: the same with the static filter on epair1a, $(date -u +%FT%TZ)"
sudo ngctl -f /tmp/m7c-ng/ng-epair1a-static.ngctl
sudo ngctl show epair1a: | sed 's/^/  host: /'
inject J2
for h in jail bridge; do echo "  ng skf_epair1a $h: $(sudo ngctl msg skf_epair1a: getstats \"$h\" | sed -n 's/^Args:[[:space:]]*//p')"; done
r 'ngctl list'
sudo ngctl shutdown skf_epair1a:
sudo ngctl show epair1a: | sed 's/^/  host after removing the victim filter: /'

######## harness file: order.sh
G=/tmp/m7c-ng/ngbpfgen; D=/tmp/m7c-ng/cap; VM=02:5e:00:0a:00:20; FM=02:5e:00:00:00:01; OM=02:5e:00:0a:00:30
echo "######## NG X1: failure handling on skf_epair0a (attacker running), $(date -u +%FT%TZ)"
set -x
sudo ngctl msg skf_epair0a: setprogram '{ thisHook="jail" ifMatch="bridge" ifNotMatch="" bpf_prog_len=2 bpf_prog=[ { code=21 jt=5 jf=0 k=0 } { code=6 jt=0 jf=0 k=0 } ] }'

sleep 4.2
$G -if $e -mac $OM -ip 192.0.2.30 > $D/X2.ngctl; sudo ngctl -f $D/X2.ngctl; echo "filter on $e at $(date -u +%T.%N | cut -c1-12)"
wait %2 2>/dev/null; sleep 5
sudo pkill -f "tcpdump -l -n -e -tttt -i epair1a"; sleep 0.5
echo "  forged frames delivered to the victim port (epair1a): $(grep -c '^20' $D/X2-epair1a.txt)"; sed 's/^/     /' $D/X2-epair1a.txt
for h in jail; do echo "  ng skf_$e $h: $(sudo ngctl msg skf_$e: getstats \"$h\" | sed -n 's/^Args:[[:space:]]*//p')"; done
sudo ifconfig bridge1 deletem $e; sudo ifconfig $e destroy
echo "  after destroy:"; sudo ngctl list | grep -E "$e|skf_" | sed 's/^/     /'
echo "######## NG X3: right order: filter on the new port before it joins bridge1, sender already running, $(date -u +%FT%TZ)"
e=$(sudo ifconfig epair create); b=${e%a}b; echo "created $e/$b"; echo $e > /tmp/m7c-ng/x3-epair
sudo ifconfig $b up
sudo tcpdump -l -n -e -tttt -i epair1a "ether src $FM or ether src $OM" > $D/X3-epair1a.txt 2>/dev/null &
sleep 1.5
sudo /tmp/m7c-ng/rawl2 -if $b -mode icmp -eth-src $FM -eth-dst $VM -src 192.0.2.50 -dst 192.0.2.20 -count 8 > $D/X3-send.txt 2>&1 &
sleep 1
$G -if $e -mac $OM -ip 192.0.2.30 > $D/X3.ngctl; sudo ngctl -f $D/X3.ngctl; echo "filter on $e at $(date -u +%T.%N | cut -c1-12)"
sudo ifconfig bridge1 addm $e; sudo ifconfig $e up; echo "addm+up $e at $(date -u +%T.%N | cut -c1-12)"
wait %2 2>/dev/null
sudo /tmp/m7c-ng/rawl2 -if $b -mode icmp -eth-src $OM -eth-dst $VM -src 192.0.2.30 -dst 192.0.2.20 -count 2 > /dev/null 2>&1
sleep 2
sudo pkill -f "tcpdump -l -n -e -tttt -i epair1a"; sleep 0.5
echo "  frames from $b delivered to the victim port (epair1a): $(grep -c '^20' $D/X3-epair1a.txt)"; sed 's/^/     /' $D/X3-epair1a.txt
echo "  sender:"; sed 's/^/     /' $D/X3-send.txt | tail -2
echo "  ng skf_$e jail: $(sudo ngctl msg skf_$e: getstats '"jail"' | sed -n 's/^Args:[[:space:]]*//p')"
sudo ngctl show $e:
